+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Build an original, periodic 40 x 40 split-reed 2/2 twill at native 2048.
+
+Requires Python 3, NumPy, SciPy and Pillow. No network access. All paths are
+relative to this script, and all outputs stay in this material directory.
+"""
+from pathlib import Path
+import hashlib
+import json
+import time
+
+import numpy as np
+from PIL import Image, ImageCms, PngImagePlugin
+from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter, map_coordinates
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+N = 2048
+STRIPS = 40
+TILE_M = 1.5
+HEIGHT_MM = 8.0
+SEED = 617803
+TARGET_RGB = np.array([178., 160., 118.], dtype=np.float32)
+SRGB = ImageCms.ImageCmsProfile(ImageCms.createProfile('sRGB')).tobytes()
+
+
+def smoothstep(a, b, x):
+    t = np.clip((x-a)/(b-a), 0., 1.)
+    return t*t*(3.-2.*t)
+
+
+def blur(a, sigma):
+    return gaussian_filter(a, sigma, mode='wrap')
+
+
+def noise(rng, shape, sigma):
+    a = blur(rng.standard_normal(shape, dtype=np.float32), sigma)
+    return (a - a.mean()) / max(float(a.std()), 1.e-6)
+
+
+def save_png(name, a, srgb=False, extra=None):
+    info = PngImagePlugin.PngInfo()
+    info.add_text('Material', 'Hand-plaited split reed; 2-over / 2-under twill; 40 strips per axis')
+    info.add_text('Physical size', '1.5 x 1.5 metres; repeat X and Y')
+    info.add_text('Color space', 'sRGB' if srgb else 'linear data; do not decode as sRGB')
+    for k, v in (extra or {}).items():
+        info.add_text(k, v)
+    kwargs = {'icc_profile': SRGB} if srgb else {}
+    Image.fromarray(a).save(ROOT / name, pnginfo=info, **kwargs)
+
+
+def rgb8(a):
+    return np.clip(np.rint(a), 0, 255).astype(np.uint8)
+
+
+def normal_from_height(h16):
+    """Central differences of the DELIVERED uint16 height; +Y is image-up."""
+    h = h16.astype(np.float32) * (HEIGHT_MM / 65535.)
+    px_mm = TILE_M * 1000. / N
+    dx = (np.roll(h, -1, 1) - np.roll(h, 1, 1)) / (2. * px_mm)
+    dy_down = (np.roll(h, -1, 0) - np.roll(h, 1, 0)) / (2. * px_mm)
+    normal = np.stack([-dx, dy_down, np.ones_like(h)], axis=-1)
+    normal /= np.linalg.norm(normal, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
+    return rgb8((normal * .5 + .5) * 255.)
+
+
+def make_atlas(rng, src):
+    """80 independent ribbon surfaces, 64 samples wide, 2048 along the fibre.
+
+    Three native-density source patches are blended around each ribbon's ring.
+    Source ribbon edges are excluded. Slow and cross-ribbon illumination is
+    removed before the residual is used as low-amplitude pigment detail.
+    Native pixel-scale procedural fibres provide independent relief and pigment.
+    """
+    bounds = np.array([0, 86, 173, 256, 338, 420, 502, 584,
+                       667, 749, 829, 909, 986, 1058, 1131, 1199, 1254])
+    bounds = bounds * src.shape[0] / 1254.
+    cy, ax = np.mgrid[:64, :N].astype(np.float32)
+    palettes = rng.normal(0, 7.2, 80)
+    palettes -= palettes.mean()
+    hue = rng.normal(0, 2., (80, 3)).astype(np.float32)
+    colour = np.empty((80, 64, N, 3), dtype=np.float32)
+    relief = np.empty((80, 64, N), dtype=np.float32)
+    fibre_saved = np.empty_like(relief)
+    for k in range(80):
+        detail = np.zeros((64, N), dtype=np.float32)
+        tint = np.zeros_like(detail)
+        for p in range(3):
+            row = int(rng.integers(0, 16))
+            margin = 13.
+            ys = bounds[row] + margin + (cy+.5)/64.*(bounds[row+1]-bounds[row]-2*margin)
+            # Each patch is active only within +/- 683 pixels of its centre.
+            # Choose a 900 px periodic source track with a 200 px crossfade.
+            phase = rng.uniform(0, 900)
+            xs = (ax + phase) % 900
+            left = map_coordinates(src.mean(2), [ys, xs+160], order=1, mode='nearest')
+            right = map_coordinates(src.mean(2), [ys, xs+1060], order=1, mode='nearest')
+            w = smoothstep(0., 190., xs)
+            track = left*w + right*(1.-w)
+            # The right sample only contributes during the first 190 pixels,
+            # where xs+1060 <=1250. Adjacent ring endpoints remain continuous.
+            track -= blur(track, (7., 70.))
+            track -= track.mean(1, keepdims=True) * .45
+            # Raised-cosine partition of unity, periodic across output ends.
+            dist = ((ax - (p/3.*N) + N/2.) % N) - N/2.
+            weight = (1.+np.cos(np.pi*np.clip(dist/(N/3.), -1., 1.)))/2.
+            detail += track * weight
+        detail *= .47
+        coarse = noise(rng, (64, N), (1.8, 39.))
+        fine = noise(rng, (64, N), (.48, 12.))
+        fine2 = noise(rng, (64, N), (.85, 3.4))
+        drift = 1.4*np.sin(2*np.pi*ax/N*3+rng.uniform(0, 6.28))
+        drift += .55*np.sin(2*np.pi*ax/N*11+rng.uniform(0, 6.28))
+        fine = map_coordinates(fine, [(cy+drift)%64, ax], order=1, mode='grid-wrap')
+        broad = noise(rng, (64, N), (9., 110.))
+        fiber = .040*coarse + .026*fine + .013*fine2
+        # Gentle short transverse nodes and small edge splits, unique per ribbon.
+        node = np.zeros_like(detail)
+        splits = np.zeros_like(detail)
+        for _ in range(int(rng.integers(1, 4))):
+            cx = rng.uniform(0, N)
+            distance = ((ax-cx+N/2.)%N)-N/2.
+            bend = (cy-32.) * rng.uniform(-.12, .12)
+            node += np.exp(-((distance+bend)/rng.uniform(.8, 1.9))**2/2.) * rng.uniform(.025, .065)
+        if rng.random() < .60:
+            cx = rng.uniform(0, N)
+            distance = ((ax-cx+N/2.)%N)-N/2.
+            taper = np.exp(-.5*(distance/rng.uniform(40, 105))**2)
+            edge_y = rng.choice([7., 56.]) + 1.1*np.sin(distance/65.)
+            splits = np.exp(-.5*((cy-edge_y)/.5)**2)*taper
+        # Stochastic lines are pigment/relief, not directional edge shading.
+        gray = detail + 2.3*coarse + 1.45*fine + .65*fine2 + 1.8*broad
+        gray += 12.*node - 5.*splits
+        colour[k] = TARGET_RGB + palettes[k]*np.array([1., .98, .85]) + hue[k]
+        colour[k] += gray[..., None] * np.array([1., .98, .85])
+        relief[k] = fiber + node - .16*splits + detail*.003
+        fibre_saved[k] = fiber
+    return colour.reshape(80*64, N, 3), relief.reshape(80*64, N), fibre_saved.reshape(80*64, N)
+
+
+def horizon_ao(h):
+    """Periodic heightfield horizon integration, twelve azimuths, eight radii."""
+    px_mm = TILE_M * 1000./N
+    ao = np.zeros_like(h)
+    for angle in np.arange(12)*2*np.pi/12:
+        slope = np.zeros_like(h)
+        seen = set()
+        for radius in [1, 2, 3, 5, 9, 16, 30, 58]:
+            dx, dy = int(round(np.cos(angle)*radius)), int(round(np.sin(angle)*radius))
+            if (dx, dy) in seen or (dx == 0 and dy == 0):
+                continue
+            seen.add((dx, dy))
+            sample = np.roll(h, (dy, dx), axis=(0, 1))
+            slope = np.maximum(slope, (sample-h)/(np.hypot(dx,dy)*px_mm))
+        ao += 1./(1.+slope*slope)
+    return np.clip(ao/12., 0., 1.)
+
+
+def main():
+    started = time.time()
+    rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
+    print('Building eighty native-density ribbon surfaces...', flush=True)
+    src = np.asarray(Image.open(ROOT/'source/generated_reed_atlas.png').convert('RGB'), dtype=np.float32)
+    atlas, grain_atlas, fine_atlas = make_atlas(rng, src)
+    # Retain full-resolution assembled source sheets for inspection and provenance.
+    save_png('source/composed_ribbon_surfaces.png', rgb8(atlas), srgb=True)
+    yy, xx = np.mgrid[:N, :N].astype(np.float32)
+    pitch = N/STRIPS
+    u = xx/pitch + .37
+    v = yy/pitch + .21
+    # Periodic deformations retain the 40-strip winding number on both axes.
+    # A broad warp and smaller irregularities avoid a machine-square basket grid.
+    u += .105*noise(rng, (N,N), 105.) + .035*noise(rng, (N,N), 23.)
+    v += .105*noise(rng, (N,N), 105.) + .035*noise(rng, (N,N), 23.)
+    column_offsets = rng.uniform(-.13, .13, 40).astype(np.float32)
+    row_offsets = rng.uniform(-.13, .13, 40).astype(np.float32)
+    u += map_coordinates(column_offsets, [(xx/pitch).ravel()], order=3, mode='grid-wrap').reshape(N,N)
+    v += map_coordinates(row_offsets, [(yy/pitch).ravel()], order=3, mode='grid-wrap').reshape(N,N)
+    ui, vi = np.floor(u).astype(np.int32), np.floor(v).astype(np.int32)
+    uf, vf = u-ui, v-vi
+    hcoords = [(vi%40)*64 + np.clip(vf*64, .51, 63.49), (u/40.*N)%N]
+    vcoords = [((ui%40)+40)*64 + np.clip(uf*64, .51, 63.49), (v/40.*N)%N]
+    print('Plaiting 40 x 40, with periodic width and alignment variation...', flush=True)
+    col_h = np.stack([map_coordinates(atlas[...,c], hcoords, order=1, mode='grid-wrap') for c in range(3)], -1)
+    col_v = np.stack([map_coordinates(atlas[...,c], vcoords, order=1, mode='grid-wrap') for c in range(3)], -1)
+    relief_h = map_coordinates(grain_atlas, hcoords, order=1, mode='grid-wrap')
+    relief_v = map_coordinates(grain_atlas, vcoords, order=1, mode='grid-wrap')
+    native_fibre_h = map_coordinates(fine_atlas, hcoords, order=1, mode='grid-wrap')
+    native_fibre_v = map_coordinates(fine_atlas, vcoords, order=1, mode='grid-wrap')
+    del atlas, grain_atlas, fine_atlas
+    wear = smoothstep(-.5, 2.0, .73*noise(rng, (N,N), 108.) + .27*noise(rng, (N,N), 39.))
+    # Ragged edges stay below a pixel on average; the weave remains intact.
+    edge_h = .25*noise(rng, (N,N), (4., 1.7)) + .23*noise(rng,(N,N), (8.,12.))
+    edge_v = .25*noise(rng, (N,N), (1.7,4.)) + .23*noise(rng,(N,N), (12.,8.))
+    dh = (np.minimum(vf,1.-vf)*pitch) - .64 + edge_h
+    dv = (np.minimum(uf,1.-uf)*pitch) - .64 + edge_v
+    cover_h = smoothstep(-.6,.6,dh)
+    cover_v = smoothstep(-.6,.6,dv)
+    phase_h = np.cos(np.pi/2.*(u-vi-1.))
+    phase_v = -np.cos(np.pi/2.*(ui-v))
+    arch_h = np.tanh(phase_h*1.7)/np.tanh(1.7)
+    arch_v = np.tanh(phase_v*1.7)/np.tanh(1.7)
+    thickness_h = rng.uniform(-.10,.10,40)[vi%40]
+    thickness_v = rng.uniform(-.10,.10,40)[ui%40]
+    # Flat ribbons, a very slight cross-section crown, soft bent exits at crossings.
+    base_z = 3.45 + .10*noise(rng,(N,N),52.)
+    h_raw = base_z + 1.28*arch_h + thickness_h + .13*np.sin(np.pi*vf)
+    v_raw = base_z + 1.28*arch_v + thickness_v + .13*np.sin(np.pi*uf)
+    h_raw -= .33*np.exp(-np.maximum(dh,0)/1.25)
+    v_raw -= .33*np.exp(-np.maximum(dv,0)/1.25)
+    h_raw += relief_h*(1.-.37*wear)
+    v_raw += relief_v*(1.-.37*wear)
+    ground_z = 1.32 + .035*noise(rng,(N,N),(.8,.8))
+    zh = ground_z + cover_h*(h_raw-ground_z)
+    zv = ground_z + cover_v*(v_raw-ground_z)
+    top_h = smoothstep(-.065,.065,zh-zv).astype(np.float32)
+    coverage = np.maximum(cover_h,cover_v)
+    height = blur(np.maximum(zh,zv).astype(np.float32), .39)
+    print('Computing heightfield cavity occlusion and native fine detail...', flush=True)
+    ao = horizon_ao(height)
+    cavity = smoothstep(.04,.39,1.-ao)
+    grit_rng = rng.random((N,N), dtype=np.float32)
+    grit = blur((grit_rng > .987).astype(np.float32),.52)*2.5*cavity
+    dust = cavity*(.28+.25*smoothstep(-1.,1.,noise(rng,(N,N),5.)))
+    base = col_h*top_h[...,None] + col_v*(1.-top_h[...,None])
+    del col_h, col_v
+    # Only real deposited dust/grit affects albedo at crossings. AO is separate.
+    base = base*coverage[...,None] + np.array([158.,145.,113.])*(1.-coverage[...,None])
+    base += wear[...,None]*np.array([3.8,4.1,4.8])
+    base = base*(1.-dust[...,None]*.10) + np.array([168.,154.,122.])*dust[...,None]*.10
+    base -= grit[...,None]*np.array([12.,11.,8.])
+    height += grit*.07
+    base += TARGET_RGB - base.mean((0,1))
+    roughness = .827 + .017*noise(rng,(N,N),9.) - .115*wear + .041*cavity
+    roughness += .010*noise(rng,(N,N),.65)
+    roughness = np.clip(roughness,.66,.92)
+    h16 = np.rint(np.clip(height/HEIGHT_MM,0,1)*65535).astype(np.uint16)
+    # Recompute AO after the tiny grit relief, using the quantized delivered height.
+    ao = horizon_ao(h16.astype(np.float32)*HEIGHT_MM/65535.)
+    save_png('mat_base.png',rgb8(base),srgb=True)
+    save_png('mat_height.png',h16,extra={'Height scale':'0..65535 = 0..8 millimetres; white is high'})
+    save_png('mat_normal.png',normal_from_height(h16),extra={'Normal convention':'OpenGL: +X right, +Y image-up, +Z out; central differences with wrap'})
+    save_png('mat_orm.png',rgb8(np.stack([ao,roughness,np.zeros_like(ao)],-1)*255),
+             extra={'Channels':'R ambient occlusion; G roughness; B metalness (zero)'})
+    # These are diagnostic fields, not additional engine textures.
+    np.savez_compressed(ROOT/'source/derivation.npz',u=u.astype(np.float32),v=v.astype(np.float32),
+        horizontal_top=top_h,wear=wear.astype(np.float32),coverage=coverage.astype(np.float32),
+        native_fibre_mm=(native_fibre_h*top_h+native_fibre_v*(1.-top_h)).astype(np.float32))
+    meta = {'seed':SEED,'resolution':[N,N],'strips_per_axis':STRIPS,'tile_metres':[TILE_M,TILE_M],
+        'pitch_mm':TILE_M*1000/STRIPS,'pixel_mm':TILE_M*1000/N,'height_range_mm':HEIGHT_MM,
+        'used_height_mm':[float(h16.min()*HEIGHT_MM/65535),float(h16.max()*HEIGHT_MM/65535)],
+        'source_size':list(Image.open(ROOT/'source/generated_reed_atlas.png').size),
+        'source_sha256':hashlib.sha256((ROOT/'source/generated_reed_atlas.png').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+        'normal_method':'wrapped central differences of delivered uint16; encode normalized (-dH/dx,+dH/drow,1)',
+        'build_seconds':round(time.time()-started,2)}
+    (ROOT/'source/build_metadata.json').write_text(json.dumps(meta,indent=2)+'\n')
+    print(json.dumps(meta,indent=2),flush=True)
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    main()
diff --git a/assets/source/core/structures/thatch/tex/mat/check_material.py b/assets/source/core/structures/thatch/tex/mat/check_material.py

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Validate the delivered maps and regenerate every visual check, offline."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import hashlib
+import json
+import struct
+
+import numpy as np
+from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
+from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+CHECK = ROOT/'checks'
+N = 2048
+TILE_M = 1.5
+HEIGHT_MM = 8.
+NAMES = ['base','height','normal','orm']
+
+
+def save_view(path, array):
+    Image.fromarray(np.clip(np.rint(array),0,255).astype(np.uint8)).save(CHECK/path)
+
+
+def sheet(items, path, columns=2, side=768):
+    rows = (len(items)+columns-1)//columns
+    canvas = Image.new('RGB',(columns*side, rows*(side+36)),(25,26,25))
+    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(canvas)
+    try:
+        font = ImageFont.truetype('/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Arial.ttf',17)
+    except OSError:
+        font = ImageFont.load_default()
+    for idx,(label,im) in enumerate(items):
+        x, y = (idx%columns)*side,(idx//columns)*(side+36)
+        draw.text((x+12,y+10),label,fill=(234,233,223),font=font)
+        canvas.paste(im.convert('RGB').resize((side,side),Image.Resampling.LANCZOS),(x,y+36))
+    canvas.save(CHECK/path,quality=94,subsampling=0)
+
+
+def seam_stats(a):
+    """All errors in encoded 8-bit-equivalent units, even for uint16 height."""
+    a = a.astype(np.float64)
+    if a.ndim == 2:
+        a = a[...,None]
+    result = {}
+    for axis,label in [(1,'left_right'),(0,'top_bottom')]:
+        wrap = np.abs(np.take(a,0,axis)-np.take(a,-1,axis))
+        interior = np.abs(np.diff(a,axis=axis))
+        other = 0 if axis == 1 else 1
+        edge_means = interior.mean(axis=(other,2))
+        mean = float(wrap.mean())
+        p95 = float(np.percentile(edge_means,95))
+        result[label] = {'wrap_mean':mean,'interior_mean':float(interior.mean()),
+            'interior_line_p50':float(np.median(edge_means)),
+            'interior_line_p95':p95,'wrap_to_interior_p95':mean/max(p95,1.e-8),
+            'wrap_pixel_p99':float(np.percentile(wrap,99)),
+            'interior_pixel_p99':float(np.percentile(interior,99)),
+            'within_interior_p95':bool(mean<=p95)}
+    return result
+
+
+def decode_srgb(s):
+    return np.where(s<=.04045,s/12.92,((s+.055)/1.055)**2.4)
+
+
+def encode_srgb(l):
+    l = np.maximum(l,0)
+    return np.where(l<=.0031308,12.92*l,1.055*l**(1/2.4)-.055)
+
+
+def main():
+    CHECK.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    maps = {name:np.array(Image.open(ROOT/f'mat_{name}.png')) for name in NAMES}
+    formats = {}
+    for name in NAMES:
+        path = ROOT/f'mat_{name}.png'
+        with Image.open(path) as im:
+            ihdr = struct.unpack('>IIBBBBB',path.read_bytes()[16:29])
+            formats[name] = {'size':list(im.size),'mode':im.mode,'bit_depth':ihdr[2],
+                'png_color_type':ihdr[3],'has_icc_profile':bool(im.info.get('icc_profile')),
+                'sha256':hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()}
+            assert im.size == (N,N), f'{name}: wrong dimensions'
+            assert ihdr[2] == (16 if name=='height' else 8), f'{name}: wrong bit depth'
+            assert ihdr[3] == (0 if name=='height' else 2), f'{name}: wrong channels'
+            assert bool(im.info.get('icc_profile')) == (name=='base'), f'{name}: colour profile'
+    base = maps['base'].astype(np.float32)
+    height = maps['height'].astype(np.float64) * HEIGHT_MM / 65535.
+    normal_raw = maps['normal'].astype(np.float64)/127.5-1.
+    lengths = np.linalg.norm(normal_raw,axis=-1)
+    normal = normal_raw/lengths[...,None]
+    orm = maps['orm'].astype(np.float64)/255.
+    # Independent reconstruction: pad the saved height, differentiate, normalize.
+    hp = np.pad(height,1,mode='wrap')
+    px_mm = TILE_M*1000/N
+    dx = (hp[1:-1,2:]-hp[1:-1,:-2])/(2*px_mm)
+    drow = (hp[2:,1:-1]-hp[:-2,1:-1])/(2*px_mm)
+    expected = np.stack([-dx,drow,np.ones_like(dx)],-1)
+    expected /= np.linalg.norm(expected,axis=-1,keepdims=True)
+    angular = np.degrees(np.arccos(np.clip((normal*expected).sum(-1),-1,1)))
+    directx = expected.copy()
+    directx[...,1] *= -1
+    wrong_g = np.degrees(np.arccos(np.clip((normal*directx).sum(-1),-1,1)))
+    assert angular.max()<.45, 'Normal does not agree with height within 8-bit quantization'
+    assert np.all(maps['orm'][...,2]==0),'Nonzero metalness'
+    mean = base.astype(np.float64).mean((0,1))
+    assert np.max(np.abs(mean-[178,160,118]))<.1,'Base colour mean missed target'
+    seam = {name:seam_stats(a*(255./65535.) if name=='height' else a) for name,a in maps.items()}
+    assert all(s['within_interior_p95'] for m in seam.values() for s in m.values()), 'A border exceeds normal interior variation'
+
+    fields = np.load(ROOT/'source/derivation.npz')
+    u,v = fields['u'],fields['v']
+    ui,vi = np.floor(u).astype(int),np.floor(v).astype(int)
+    label = (vi%40)*40+(ui%40)
+    centre_distance = (u-ui-.5)**2+(v-vi-.5)**2
+    mins = np.full(1600,np.inf)
+    np.minimum.at(mins,label.ravel(),centre_distance.ravel())
+    centres = centre_distance==mins[label]
+    expected_top = ((ui-vi)%4)<2
+    wrong_centres = np.count_nonzero((fields['horizontal_top'][centres]>.5)!=expected_top[centres])
+    assert np.count_nonzero(centres)==1600 and wrong_centres==0,'Incorrect 2/2 crossing order'
+    # Central differences on the unwrapped coordinates, excluding wrap jumps.
+    du = np.diff(u,axis=1)
+    dv = np.diff(v,axis=0)
+    assert du.min()>0 and dv.min()>0,'Warp folds or reverses strips'
+    wear = fields['wear'].ravel()[::7]
+    wear_correlation = float(np.corrcoef(wear,orm[...,1].ravel()[::7])[0,1])
+    assert wear_correlation<-.6,'Worn areas should be smoother'
+    cavity = gaussian_filter(height,3.,mode='wrap')-height
+    cavity_ao = float(orm[...,0][cavity>.2].mean())
+    flat_ao = float(orm[...,0][np.abs(cavity)<.015].mean())
+    assert cavity_ao<flat_ao,'Cavity AO appears inverted'
+    metrics = {'physical':{'tile_metres':[TILE_M,TILE_M],'resolution':[N,N],
+        'strip_count_each_axis':40,'nominal_strip_pitch_mm':37.5,'pixel_mm':px_mm,
+        'full_height_range_mm':HEIGHT_MM,'actual_height_min_max_mm':[float(height.min()),float(height.max())]},
+        'formats':formats,'base_mean_srgb':mean.tolist(),
+        'base_channel_min_max':[[int(base[...,i].min()),int(base[...,i].max())] for i in range(3)],
+        'wrap_seams_8bit_equivalent':seam,
+        'normal_height_agreement_degrees':{'mean':float(angular.mean()),'p99':float(np.percentile(angular,99)),
+            'max':float(angular.max()),'wrong_green_convention_mean':float(wrong_g.mean())},
+        'decoded_normal_length':{'mean':float(lengths.mean()),'max_error':float(np.max(np.abs(lengths-1)))},
+        'weave':{'verified_crossings':int(np.count_nonzero(centres)),'wrong_crossings':int(wrong_centres),
+            'pattern':'horizontal on top iff (column - row) mod 4 is 0 or 1; both axes 40',
+            'minimum_grid_jacobian_diagonals':[float(du.min()),float(dv.min())]},
+        'roughness':{'min':float(orm[...,1].min()),'max':float(orm[...,1].max()),
+            'mean':float(orm[...,1].mean()),'wear_correlation':wear_correlation},
+        'ao':{'min':float(orm[...,0].min()),'mean':float(orm[...,0].mean()),
+            'cavity_mean':cavity_ao,'flat_mean':flat_ao},
+        'metalness_nonzero_pixels':int(np.count_nonzero(maps['orm'][...,2])),
+        'native_detail':{'procedural_fibre_rms_mm':float(fields['native_fibre_mm'].std()),
+            'base_highpass_rms_1px':float((base-gaussian_filter(base,(1.,1.,0.),mode='wrap')).std())},
+        'checks_passed':True}
+    (CHECK/'metrics.json').write_text(json.dumps(metrics,indent=2)+'\n')
+    display = {name:Image.fromarray((a/257.).astype(np.uint8) if name=='height' else a) for name,a in maps.items()}
+    sheet([(f'mat_{name} | '+('sRGB' if name=='base' else 'linear data'),im) for name,im in display.items()],
+          'layers.jpg')
+    offsets=[]
+    for name,a in maps.items():
+        off=np.roll(a,(N//2,N//2),axis=(0,1))
+        Image.fromarray(off).save(CHECK/f'{name}_half_offset.png')
+        off_view=Image.fromarray((off/257.).astype(np.uint8) if name=='height' else off)
+        offsets.append((f'{name} | wrap offset 1024,1024',off_view))
+    sheet(offsets,'offsets.jpg')
+    tile=Image.fromarray(np.tile(maps['base'],(3,3,1)))
+    tile.save(CHECK/'base_tiled_3x3.png')
+    tile.resize((1920,1920),Image.Resampling.LANCZOS).save(CHECK/'tilings.jpg',quality=95,subsampling=0)
+    crop=(704,704,1344,1344)
+    display['base'].crop(crop).save(CHECK/'base_crop_1to1.png')
+    display['normal'].crop(crop).save(CHECK/'normal_crop_1to1.png')
+    sheet([(f'{name} | 1:1, x704 y704, 640px',display[name].crop(crop)) for name in ['base','normal']],
+          'detail_1to1.png',side=640)
+    sheet([(f'{name} | aligned 640px crop',display[name].crop(crop)) for name in NAMES],
+          'alignment.jpg',side=640)
+    # Relighting is diagnostic. The delivered base contains none of this light.
+    linear=decode_srgb(base/255.)
+    lighting=[]
+    lights=[('upper left',[-.63,.45,.64]),('lower right',[.57,-.55,.61]),('upper grazing',[.1,.86,.5])]
+    for label,vec in lights:
+        light=np.array(vec)/np.linalg.norm(vec)
+        ndotl=np.maximum(0.,(normal*light).sum(-1))
+        shade=.28*orm[...,0]+.91*ndotl*(.75+.25*orm[...,0])
+        lit=encode_srgb(linear*shade[...,None])
+        lit=Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.clip(np.rint(lit*255),0,255)))
+        lit.save(CHECK/('relit_'+label.replace(' ','_')+'.png'))
+        lighting.append((label+' | full tile',lit))
+    lighting.extend([(label+' | 1:1 detail',im.crop(crop)) for label,im in lighting.copy()])
+    sheet(lighting,'relighting.jpg',columns=3,side=640)
+    # Neutral clay views expose the crossing shape independently of pigmentation.
+    clay=[]
+    for label,vec in lights[:2]:
+        light=np.array(vec)/np.linalg.norm(vec)
+        shade=.25*orm[...,0]+.72*np.maximum(0.,(normal*light).sum(-1))
+        im=Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.clip(encode_srgb(shade)*220,0,255))).convert('RGB')
+        clay.append((label+' | height-derived normals + AO',im.crop(crop)))
+    sheet(clay,'clay_crossings.jpg',side=640)
+    print(json.dumps({k:metrics[k] for k in ['base_mean_srgb','normal_height_agreement_degrees',
+        'weave','roughness','ao','checks_passed']},indent=2))
+    print('Checks and previews saved in',CHECK)
+
+
+if __name__=='__main__':
+    main()

+Use case: photorealistic-natural.
+Asset type: original surface-detail source atlas for a survival game's dry split-reed floor material. This is NOT a finished woven mat; it is a sampling sheet of long flat reed ribbons.
+Primary request: a square, extremely detailed, orthographic straight-down scan of sixteen long, flattened split-reed / rush strips lying parallel horizontally, each going all the way from left to right edge. Fill the square. All fibres follow the horizontal long axis. Each ribbon has a slightly different natural cream, pale khaki, gray-beige or light tan pigment. Dry pale reed, desaturated, no green or orange. Subtle fibrous striations, occasional tiny transverse node creases and thin fraying at a few ribbon edges, fine flaking pith, slight abrasions, naturally uneven longitudinal pigment. Substantial fine real organic detail everywhere, not smooth or rendered plastic. Restrained variation, no distinctive dark marks, no holes, no rot.
+Composition: exactly overhead, flat, edge-to-edge crop of parallel horizontal split-reed ribbons; no perspective. No weave, no crossing ribbons, no borders, no binding, no props, no text.
+Lighting: diffuse cross-polarized scan, absolutely flat albedo-like surface, no directional lighting, no highlights, no cast shadows, no ambient occlusion between ribbons. Texture pigmentation only. Approximate mean sRGB 178,160,118. High resolution fine surface detail. Original invented material; do not reference any existing game.

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..ff94cba94a2716248d5c13a81e3b74eb0df6d677

+++ b/assets/source/core/structures/thatch/tex/mat/build_material.py
@@ -0,0 +1,264 @@
+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Build an original, periodic 40 x 40 split-reed 2/2 twill at native 2048.
+
+Requires Python 3, NumPy, SciPy and Pillow. No network access. All paths are
+relative to this script, and all outputs stay in this material directory.
+"""
+from pathlib import Path
+import hashlib
+import json
+import time
+
+import numpy as np
+from PIL import Image, ImageCms, PngImagePlugin
+from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter, map_coordinates
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+N = 2048
+STRIPS = 40
+TILE_M = 1.5
+HEIGHT_MM = 8.0
+SEED = 617803
+TARGET_RGB = np.array([178., 160., 118.], dtype=np.float32)
+SRGB = ImageCms.ImageCmsProfile(ImageCms.createProfile('sRGB')).tobytes()
+
+
+def smoothstep(a, b, x):
+    t = np.clip((x-a)/(b-a), 0., 1.)
+    return t*t*(3.-2.*t)
+
+
+def blur(a, sigma):
+    return gaussian_filter(a, sigma, mode='wrap')
+
+
+def noise(rng, shape, sigma):
+    a = blur(rng.standard_normal(shape, dtype=np.float32), sigma)
+    return (a - a.mean()) / max(float(a.std()), 1.e-6)
+
+
+def save_png(name, a, srgb=False, extra=None):
+    info = PngImagePlugin.PngInfo()
+    info.add_text('Material', 'Hand-plaited split reed; 2-over / 2-under twill; 40 strips per axis')
+    info.add_text('Physical size', '1.5 x 1.5 metres; repeat X and Y')
+    info.add_text('Color space', 'sRGB' if srgb else 'linear data; do not decode as sRGB')
+    for k, v in (extra or {}).items():
+        info.add_text(k, v)
+    kwargs = {'icc_profile': SRGB} if srgb else {}
+    Image.fromarray(a).save(ROOT / name, pnginfo=info, **kwargs)
+
+
+def rgb8(a):
+    return np.clip(np.rint(a), 0, 255).astype(np.uint8)
+
+
+def normal_from_height(h16):
+    """Central differences of the DELIVERED uint16 height; +Y is image-up."""
+    h = h16.astype(np.float32) * (HEIGHT_MM / 65535.)
+    px_mm = TILE_M * 1000. / N
+    dx = (np.roll(h, -1, 1) - np.roll(h, 1, 1)) / (2. * px_mm)
+    dy_down = (np.roll(h, -1, 0) - np.roll(h, 1, 0)) / (2. * px_mm)
+    normal = np.stack([-dx, dy_down, np.ones_like(h)], axis=-1)
+    normal /= np.linalg.norm(normal, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
+    return rgb8((normal * .5 + .5) * 255.)
+
+
+def make_atlas(rng, src):
+    """80 independent ribbon surfaces, 64 samples wide, 2048 along the fibre.
+
+    Three native-density source patches are blended around each ribbon's ring.
+    Source ribbon edges are excluded. Slow and cross-ribbon illumination is
+    removed before the residual is used as low-amplitude pigment detail.
+    Native pixel-scale procedural fibres provide independent relief and pigment.
+    """
+    bounds = np.array([0, 86, 173, 256, 338, 420, 502, 584,
+                       667, 749, 829, 909, 986, 1058, 1131, 1199, 1254])
+    bounds = bounds * src.shape[0] / 1254.
+    cy, ax = np.mgrid[:64, :N].astype(np.float32)
+    palettes = rng.normal(0, 4.8, 80)
+    palettes -= palettes.mean()
+    hue = rng.normal(0, 1.3, (80, 3)).astype(np.float32)
+    colour = np.empty((80, 64, N, 3), dtype=np.float32)
+    relief = np.empty((80, 64, N), dtype=np.float32)
+    fibre_saved = np.empty_like(relief)
+    for k in range(80):
+        detail = np.zeros((64, N), dtype=np.float32)
+        for p in range(3):
+            row = int(rng.integers(0, 16))
+            margin = 13.
+            ys = bounds[row] + margin + (cy+.5)/64.*(bounds[row+1]-bounds[row]-2*margin)
+            # Each patch is active only within +/- 683 pixels of its centre.
+            # Choose a 900 px periodic source track with a 200 px crossfade.
+            phase = rng.uniform(0, 900)
+            xs = (ax + phase) % 900
+            left = map_coordinates(src.mean(2), [ys, xs+160], order=1, mode='nearest')
+            right = map_coordinates(src.mean(2), [ys, xs+1060], order=1, mode='nearest')
+            w = smoothstep(0., 190., xs)
+            track = left*w + right*(1.-w)
+            # The right sample only contributes during the first 190 pixels,
+            # where xs+1060 <=1250. Adjacent ring endpoints remain continuous.
+            track -= blur(track, (7., 70.))
+            track -= track.mean(1, keepdims=True) * .45
+            # Raised-cosine partition of unity, periodic across output ends.
+            dist = ((ax - (p/3.*N) + N/2.) % N) - N/2.
+            weight = (1.+np.cos(np.pi*np.clip(dist/(N/3.), -1., 1.)))/2.
+            detail += track * weight
+        detail *= .65
+        coarse = noise(rng, (64, N), (1.8, 39.))
+        fine = noise(rng, (64, N), (.48, 12.))
+        fine2 = noise(rng, (64, N), (.85, 3.4))
+        drift = 1.4*np.sin(2*np.pi*ax/N*3+rng.uniform(0, 6.28))
+        drift += .55*np.sin(2*np.pi*ax/N*11+rng.uniform(0, 6.28))
+        fine = map_coordinates(fine, [(cy+drift)%64, ax], order=1, mode='grid-wrap')
+        broad = noise(rng, (64, N), (9., 110.))
+        local_tone = noise(rng, (64, N), (22., 135.))
+        fiber = .040*coarse + .026*fine + .013*fine2
+        # Gentle short transverse nodes and small edge splits, unique per ribbon.
+        node = np.zeros_like(detail)
+        splits = np.zeros_like(detail)
+        raised_fibre = np.zeros_like(detail)
+        for _ in range(int(rng.integers(1, 4))):
+            cx = rng.uniform(0, N)
+            distance = ((ax-cx+N/2.)%N)-N/2.
+            bend = (cy-32.) * rng.uniform(-.12, .12)
+            node += np.exp(-((distance+bend)/rng.uniform(.8, 1.9))**2/2.) * rng.uniform(.025, .065)
+        for _ in range(int(rng.integers(0, 4))):
+            cx = rng.uniform(0, N)
+            distance = ((ax-cx+N/2.)%N)-N/2.
+            taper = np.exp(-.5*(distance/rng.uniform(25, 100))**2)
+            edge_y = rng.choice([3.3, 59.7]) + 1.1*np.sin(distance/65.)
+            width = rng.uniform(.45, .85)
+            splits += np.exp(-.5*((cy-edge_y)/width)**2)*taper
+            outward = -1.7 if float(edge_y[0,0])<32 else 1.7
+            raised_fibre += np.exp(-.5*((cy-edge_y-outward)/.55)**2)*taper
+        # Stochastic lines are pigment/relief, not directional edge shading.
+        gray = detail + 1.7*coarse + 1.65*fine + .85*fine2 + 2.1*broad + 4.6*local_tone
+        gray += 12.*node - 9.*splits + 3.5*raised_fibre
+        colour[k] = TARGET_RGB + palettes[k]*np.array([1., .98, .85]) + hue[k]
+        colour[k] += gray[..., None] * np.array([1., .98, .85])
+        relief[k] = fiber + node - .24*splits + .18*raised_fibre + detail*.003
+        fibre_saved[k] = fiber
+    return colour.reshape(80*64, N, 3), relief.reshape(80*64, N), fibre_saved.reshape(80*64, N)
+
+
+def horizon_ao(h):
+    """Periodic heightfield horizon integration, twelve azimuths, eight radii."""
+    px_mm = TILE_M * 1000./N
+    ao = np.zeros_like(h)
+    for angle in np.arange(12)*2*np.pi/12:
+        slope = np.zeros_like(h)
+        seen = set()
+        for radius in [1, 2, 3, 5, 9, 16, 30, 58]:
+            dx, dy = int(round(np.cos(angle)*radius)), int(round(np.sin(angle)*radius))
+            if (dx, dy) in seen or (dx == 0 and dy == 0):
+                continue
+            seen.add((dx, dy))
+            sample = np.roll(h, (dy, dx), axis=(0, 1))
+            slope = np.maximum(slope, (sample-h)/(np.hypot(dx,dy)*px_mm))
+        ao += 1./(1.+slope*slope)
+    return np.clip(ao/12., 0., 1.)
+
+
+def main():
+    started = time.time()
+    rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
+    print('Building eighty native-density ribbon surfaces...', flush=True)
+    src = np.asarray(Image.open(ROOT/'source/generated_reed_atlas.png').convert('RGB'), dtype=np.float32)
+    atlas, grain_atlas, fine_atlas = make_atlas(rng, src)
+    # Retain full-resolution assembled source sheets for inspection and provenance.
+    save_png('source/composed_ribbon_surfaces.png', rgb8(atlas), srgb=True)
+    yy, xx = np.mgrid[:N, :N].astype(np.float32)
+    pitch = N/STRIPS
+    u = xx/pitch + .37
+    v = yy/pitch + .21
+    # Periodic deformations retain the 40-strip winding number on both axes.
+    # A broad warp and smaller irregularities avoid a machine-square basket grid.
+    u += .105*noise(rng, (N,N), 105.) + .035*noise(rng, (N,N), 23.)
+    v += .105*noise(rng, (N,N), 105.) + .035*noise(rng, (N,N), 23.)
+    column_offsets = rng.uniform(-.13, .13, 40).astype(np.float32)
+    row_offsets = rng.uniform(-.13, .13, 40).astype(np.float32)
+    u += map_coordinates(column_offsets, [(xx/pitch).ravel()], order=3, mode='grid-wrap').reshape(N,N)
+    v += map_coordinates(row_offsets, [(yy/pitch).ravel()], order=3, mode='grid-wrap').reshape(N,N)
+    ui, vi = np.floor(u).astype(np.int32), np.floor(v).astype(np.int32)
+    uf, vf = u-ui, v-vi
+    hcoords = [(vi%40)*64 + np.clip(vf*64, .51, 63.49), (u/40.*N)%N]
+    vcoords = [((ui%40)+40)*64 + np.clip(uf*64, .51, 63.49), (v/40.*N)%N]
+    print('Plaiting 40 x 40, with periodic width and alignment variation...', flush=True)
+    col_h = np.stack([map_coordinates(atlas[...,c], hcoords, order=1, mode='grid-wrap') for c in range(3)], -1)
+    col_v = np.stack([map_coordinates(atlas[...,c], vcoords, order=1, mode='grid-wrap') for c in range(3)], -1)
+    relief_h = map_coordinates(grain_atlas, hcoords, order=1, mode='grid-wrap')
+    relief_v = map_coordinates(grain_atlas, vcoords, order=1, mode='grid-wrap')
+    native_fibre_h = map_coordinates(fine_atlas, hcoords, order=1, mode='grid-wrap')
+    native_fibre_v = map_coordinates(fine_atlas, vcoords, order=1, mode='grid-wrap')
+    del atlas, grain_atlas, fine_atlas
+    wear = smoothstep(-.5, 2.0, .73*noise(rng, (N,N), 108.) + .27*noise(rng, (N,N), 39.))
+    # Ragged edges stay below a pixel on average; the weave remains intact.
+    edge_h = .25*noise(rng, (N,N), (4., 1.7)) + .23*noise(rng,(N,N), (8.,12.))
+    edge_v = .25*noise(rng, (N,N), (1.7,4.)) + .23*noise(rng,(N,N), (12.,8.))
+    dh = (np.minimum(vf,1.-vf)*pitch) - .64 + edge_h
+    dv = (np.minimum(uf,1.-uf)*pitch) - .64 + edge_v
+    cover_h = smoothstep(-.6,.6,dh)
+    cover_v = smoothstep(-.6,.6,dv)
+    phase_h = np.cos(np.pi/2.*(u-vi-1.))
+    phase_v = -np.cos(np.pi/2.*(ui-v))
+    arch_h = np.tanh(phase_h*1.7)/np.tanh(1.7)
+    arch_v = np.tanh(phase_v*1.7)/np.tanh(1.7)
+    thickness_h = rng.uniform(-.10,.10,40)[vi%40]
+    thickness_v = rng.uniform(-.10,.10,40)[ui%40]
+    # Flat ribbons, a very slight cross-section crown, soft bent exits at crossings.
+    base_z = 3.45 + .10*noise(rng,(N,N),52.)
+    h_raw = base_z + 1.28*arch_h + thickness_h + .13*np.sin(np.pi*vf)
+    v_raw = base_z + 1.28*arch_v + thickness_v + .13*np.sin(np.pi*uf)
+    h_raw -= .33*np.exp(-np.maximum(dh,0)/1.25)
+    v_raw -= .33*np.exp(-np.maximum(dv,0)/1.25)
+    h_raw += relief_h*(1.-.37*wear)
+    v_raw += relief_v*(1.-.37*wear)
+    ground_z = 1.32 + .035*noise(rng,(N,N),(.8,.8))
+    zh = ground_z + cover_h*(h_raw-ground_z)
+    zv = ground_z + cover_v*(v_raw-ground_z)
+    top_h = smoothstep(-.065,.065,zh-zv).astype(np.float32)
+    coverage = np.maximum(cover_h,cover_v)
+    height = blur(np.maximum(zh,zv).astype(np.float32), .39)
+    print('Computing heightfield cavity occlusion and native fine detail...', flush=True)
+    ao = horizon_ao(height)
+    cavity = smoothstep(.04,.39,1.-ao)
+    grit_rng = rng.random((N,N), dtype=np.float32)
+    grit = blur((grit_rng > .987).astype(np.float32),.52)*2.5*cavity
+    dust = cavity*(.28+.25*smoothstep(-1.,1.,noise(rng,(N,N),5.)))
+    base = col_h*top_h[...,None] + col_v*(1.-top_h[...,None])
+    del col_h, col_v
+    # Only real deposited dust/grit affects albedo at crossings. AO is separate.
+    base = base*coverage[...,None] + np.array([158.,145.,113.])*(1.-coverage[...,None])
+    base += wear[...,None]*np.array([3.8,4.1,4.8])
+    base = base*(1.-dust[...,None]*.10) + np.array([168.,154.,122.])*dust[...,None]*.10
+    base -= grit[...,None]*np.array([12.,11.,8.])
+    height += grit*.07
+    base += TARGET_RGB - base.mean((0,1))
+    roughness = .827 + .017*noise(rng,(N,N),9.) - .115*wear + .041*cavity
+    roughness += .010*noise(rng,(N,N),.65)
+    roughness = np.clip(roughness,.66,.92)
+    h16 = np.rint(np.clip(height/HEIGHT_MM,0,1)*65535).astype(np.uint16)
+    # Recompute AO after the tiny grit relief, using the quantized delivered height.
+    ao = horizon_ao(h16.astype(np.float32)*HEIGHT_MM/65535.)
+    save_png('mat_base.png',rgb8(base),srgb=True)
+    save_png('mat_height.png',h16,extra={'Height scale':'0..65535 = 0..8 millimetres; white is high'})
+    save_png('mat_normal.png',normal_from_height(h16),extra={'Normal convention':'OpenGL: +X right, +Y image-up, +Z out; central differences with wrap'})
+    save_png('mat_orm.png',rgb8(np.stack([ao,roughness,np.zeros_like(ao)],-1)*255),
+             extra={'Channels':'R ambient occlusion; G roughness; B metalness (zero)'})
+    # These are diagnostic fields, not additional engine textures.
+    np.savez_compressed(ROOT/'source/derivation.npz',u=u.astype(np.float32),v=v.astype(np.float32),
+        horizontal_top=top_h,wear=wear.astype(np.float32),coverage=coverage.astype(np.float32),
+        native_fibre_mm=(native_fibre_h*top_h+native_fibre_v*(1.-top_h)).astype(np.float32))
+    meta = {'seed':SEED,'resolution':[N,N],'strips_per_axis':STRIPS,'tile_metres':[TILE_M,TILE_M],
+        'pitch_mm':TILE_M*1000/STRIPS,'pixel_mm':TILE_M*1000/N,'height_range_mm':HEIGHT_MM,
+        'used_height_mm':[float(h16.min()*HEIGHT_MM/65535),float(h16.max()*HEIGHT_MM/65535)],
+        'source_size':list(Image.open(ROOT/'source/generated_reed_atlas.png').size),
+        'source_sha256':hashlib.sha256((ROOT/'source/generated_reed_atlas.png').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+        'normal_method':'wrapped central differences of delivered uint16; encode normalized (-dH/dx,+dH/drow,1)',
+        'build_seconds':round(time.time()-started,2)}
+    (ROOT/'source/build_metadata.json').write_text(json.dumps(meta,indent=2)+'\n')
+    print(json.dumps(meta,indent=2),flush=True)
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    main()

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Validate the delivered maps and regenerate every visual check, offline."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import hashlib
+import json
+import struct
+
+import numpy as np
+from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
+from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+CHECK = ROOT/'checks'
+N = 2048
+TILE_M = 1.5
+HEIGHT_MM = 8.
+NAMES = ['base','height','normal','orm']
+
+
+def save_view(path, array):
+    Image.fromarray(np.clip(np.rint(array),0,255).astype(np.uint8)).save(CHECK/path)
+
+
+def sheet(items, path, columns=2, side=768):
+    rows = (len(items)+columns-1)//columns
+    canvas = Image.new('RGB',(columns*side, rows*(side+36)),(25,26,25))
+    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(canvas)
+    try:
+        font = ImageFont.truetype('/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Arial.ttf',17)
+    except OSError:
+        font = ImageFont.load_default()
+    for idx,(label,im) in enumerate(items):
+        x, y = (idx%columns)*side,(idx//columns)*(side+36)
+        draw.text((x+12,y+10),label,fill=(234,233,223),font=font)
+        canvas.paste(im.convert('RGB').resize((side,side),Image.Resampling.LANCZOS),(x,y+36))
+    canvas.save(CHECK/path,quality=94,subsampling=0)
+
+
+def seam_stats(a):
+    """All errors in encoded 8-bit-equivalent units, even for uint16 height."""
+    a = a.astype(np.float64)
+    if a.ndim == 2:
+        a = a[...,None]
+    result = {}
+    for axis,label in [(1,'left_right'),(0,'top_bottom')]:
+        wrap = np.abs(np.take(a,0,axis)-np.take(a,-1,axis))
+        interior = np.abs(np.diff(a,axis=axis))
+        other = 0 if axis == 1 else 1
+        edge_means = interior.mean(axis=(other,2))
+        mean = float(wrap.mean())
+        p95 = float(np.percentile(edge_means,95))
+        result[label] = {'wrap_mean':mean,'interior_mean':float(interior.mean()),
+            'interior_line_p50':float(np.median(edge_means)),
+            'interior_line_p95':p95,'wrap_to_interior_p95':mean/max(p95,1.e-8),
+            'wrap_pixel_p99':float(np.percentile(wrap,99)),
+            'interior_pixel_p99':float(np.percentile(interior,99)),
+            'within_interior_p95':bool(mean<=p95)}
+    return result
+
+
+def decode_srgb(s):
+    return np.where(s<=.04045,s/12.92,((s+.055)/1.055)**2.4)
+
+
+def encode_srgb(l):
+    l = np.maximum(l,0)
+    return np.where(l<=.0031308,12.92*l,1.055*l**(1/2.4)-.055)
+
+
+def main():
+    CHECK.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    maps = {name:np.array(Image.open(ROOT/f'mat_{name}.png')) for name in NAMES}
+    formats = {}
+    for name in NAMES:
+        path = ROOT/f'mat_{name}.png'
+        with Image.open(path) as im:
+            ihdr = struct.unpack('>IIBBBBB',path.read_bytes()[16:29])
+            formats[name] = {'size':list(im.size),'mode':im.mode,'bit_depth':ihdr[2],
+                'png_color_type':ihdr[3],'has_icc_profile':bool(im.info.get('icc_profile')),
+                'sha256':hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()}
+            assert im.size == (N,N), f'{name}: wrong dimensions'
+            assert ihdr[2] == (16 if name=='height' else 8), f'{name}: wrong bit depth'
+            assert ihdr[3] == (0 if name=='height' else 2), f'{name}: wrong channels'
+            assert bool(im.info.get('icc_profile')) == (name=='base'), f'{name}: colour profile'
+    base = maps['base'].astype(np.float32)
+    height = maps['height'].astype(np.float64) * HEIGHT_MM / 65535.
+    normal_raw = maps['normal'].astype(np.float64)/127.5-1.
+    lengths = np.linalg.norm(normal_raw,axis=-1)
+    normal = normal_raw/lengths[...,None]
+    orm = maps['orm'].astype(np.float64)/255.
+    # Independent reconstruction: pad the saved height, differentiate, normalize.
+    hp = np.pad(height,1,mode='wrap')
+    px_mm = TILE_M*1000/N
+    dx = (hp[1:-1,2:]-hp[1:-1,:-2])/(2*px_mm)
+    drow = (hp[2:,1:-1]-hp[:-2,1:-1])/(2*px_mm)
+    expected = np.stack([-dx,drow,np.ones_like(dx)],-1)
+    expected /= np.linalg.norm(expected,axis=-1,keepdims=True)
+    angular = np.degrees(np.arccos(np.clip((normal*expected).sum(-1),-1,1)))
+    directx = expected.copy()
+    directx[...,1] *= -1
+    wrong_g = np.degrees(np.arccos(np.clip((normal*directx).sum(-1),-1,1)))
+    assert angular.max()<.45, 'Normal does not agree with height within 8-bit quantization'
+    assert np.all(maps['orm'][...,2]==0),'Nonzero metalness'
+    mean = base.astype(np.float64).mean((0,1))
+    assert np.max(np.abs(mean-[178,160,118]))<.1,'Base colour mean missed target'
+    seam = {name:seam_stats(a*(255./65535.) if name=='height' else a) for name,a in maps.items()}
+    assert all(s['within_interior_p95'] for m in seam.values() for s in m.values()), 'A border exceeds normal interior variation'
+
+    fields = np.load(ROOT/'source/derivation.npz')
+    u,v = fields['u'],fields['v']
+    ui,vi = np.floor(u).astype(int),np.floor(v).astype(int)
+    label = (vi%40)*40+(ui%40)
+    centre_distance = (u-ui-.5)**2+(v-vi-.5)**2
+    mins = np.full(1600,np.inf)
+    np.minimum.at(mins,label.ravel(),centre_distance.ravel())
+    centres = centre_distance==mins[label]
+    expected_top = ((ui-vi)%4)<2
+    wrong_centres = np.count_nonzero((fields['horizontal_top'][centres]>.5)!=expected_top[centres])
+    assert np.count_nonzero(centres)==1600 and wrong_centres==0,'Incorrect 2/2 crossing order'
+    # Central differences on the unwrapped coordinates, excluding wrap jumps.
+    du = np.diff(u,axis=1)
+    dv = np.diff(v,axis=0)
+    assert du.min()>0 and dv.min()>0,'Warp folds or reverses strips'
+    wear = fields['wear'].ravel()[::7]
+    wear_correlation = float(np.corrcoef(wear,orm[...,1].ravel()[::7])[0,1])
+    assert wear_correlation<-.6,'Worn areas should be smoother'
+    cavity = gaussian_filter(height,3.,mode='wrap')-height
+    cavity_ao = float(orm[...,0][cavity>.2].mean())
+    flat_ao = float(orm[...,0][np.abs(cavity)<.015].mean())
+    assert cavity_ao<flat_ao,'Cavity AO appears inverted'
+    metrics = {'physical':{'tile_metres':[TILE_M,TILE_M],'resolution':[N,N],
+        'strip_count_each_axis':40,'nominal_strip_pitch_mm':37.5,'pixel_mm':px_mm,
+        'full_height_range_mm':HEIGHT_MM,'actual_height_min_max_mm':[float(height.min()),float(height.max())]},
+        'formats':formats,'base_mean_srgb':mean.tolist(),
+        'base_channel_min_max':[[int(base[...,i].min()),int(base[...,i].max())] for i in range(3)],
+        'wrap_seams_8bit_equivalent':seam,
+        'normal_height_agreement_degrees':{'mean':float(angular.mean()),'p99':float(np.percentile(angular,99)),
+            'max':float(angular.max()),'wrong_green_convention_mean':float(wrong_g.mean())},
+        'decoded_normal_length':{'mean':float(lengths.mean()),'max_error':float(np.max(np.abs(lengths-1)))},
+        'weave':{'verified_crossings':int(np.count_nonzero(centres)),'wrong_crossings':int(wrong_centres),
+            'pattern':'horizontal on top iff (column - row) mod 4 is 0 or 1; both axes 40',
+            'minimum_grid_jacobian_diagonals':[float(du.min()),float(dv.min())]},
+        'roughness':{'min':float(orm[...,1].min()),'max':float(orm[...,1].max()),
+            'mean':float(orm[...,1].mean()),'wear_correlation':wear_correlation},
+        'ao':{'min':float(orm[...,0].min()),'mean':float(orm[...,0].mean()),
+            'cavity_mean':cavity_ao,'flat_mean':flat_ao},
+        'metalness_nonzero_pixels':int(np.count_nonzero(maps['orm'][...,2])),
+        'native_detail':{'procedural_fibre_rms_mm':float(fields['native_fibre_mm'].std()),
+            'base_highpass_rms_1px':float((base-gaussian_filter(base,(1.,1.,0.),mode='wrap')).std())},
+        'checks_passed':True}
+    (CHECK/'metrics.json').write_text(json.dumps(metrics,indent=2)+'\n')
+    display = {name:Image.fromarray((a/257.).astype(np.uint8) if name=='height' else a) for name,a in maps.items()}
+    sheet([(f'mat_{name} | '+('sRGB' if name=='base' else 'linear data'),im) for name,im in display.items()],
+          'layers.jpg')
+    offsets=[]
+    for name,a in maps.items():
+        off=np.roll(a,(N//2,N//2),axis=(0,1))
+        Image.fromarray(off).save(CHECK/f'{name}_half_offset.png')
+        off_view=Image.fromarray((off/257.).astype(np.uint8) if name=='height' else off)
+        offsets.append((f'{name} | wrap offset 1024,1024',off_view))
+    sheet(offsets,'offsets.jpg')
+    tile=Image.fromarray(np.tile(maps['base'],(3,3,1)))
+    tile.save(CHECK/'base_tiled_3x3.png')
+    tile.resize((1920,1920),Image.Resampling.LANCZOS).save(CHECK/'tilings.jpg',quality=95,subsampling=0)
+    crop=(704,704,1344,1344)
+    display['base'].crop(crop).save(CHECK/'base_crop_1to1.png')
+    display['normal'].crop(crop).save(CHECK/'normal_crop_1to1.png')
+    sheet([(f'{name} | 1:1, x704 y704, 640px',display[name].crop(crop)) for name in ['base','normal']],
+          'detail_1to1.png',side=640)
+    sheet([(f'{name} | aligned 640px crop',display[name].crop(crop)) for name in NAMES],
+          'alignment.jpg',side=640)
+    # Relighting is diagnostic. The delivered base contains none of this light.
+    linear=decode_srgb(base/255.)
+    lighting=[]
+    lights=[('upper left',[-.63,.45,.64]),('lower right',[.57,-.55,.61]),('upper grazing',[.1,.86,.5])]
+    for label,vec in lights:
+        light=np.array(vec)/np.linalg.norm(vec)
+        ndotl=np.maximum(0.,(normal*light).sum(-1))
+        shade=.28*orm[...,0]+.91*ndotl*(.75+.25*orm[...,0])
+        lit=encode_srgb(linear*shade[...,None])
+        lit=Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.clip(np.rint(lit*255),0,255)))
+        lit.save(CHECK/('relit_'+label.replace(' ','_')+'.png'))
+        lighting.append((label+' | full tile',lit))
+    lighting.extend([(label+' | 1:1 detail',im.crop(crop)) for label,im in lighting.copy()])
+    sheet(lighting,'relighting.jpg',columns=3,side=640)
+    # Neutral clay views expose the crossing shape independently of pigmentation.
+    clay=[]
+    for label,vec in lights[:2]:
+        light=np.array(vec)/np.linalg.norm(vec)
+        shade=.25*orm[...,0]+.72*np.maximum(0.,(normal*light).sum(-1))
+        im=Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.clip(encode_srgb(shade)*220,0,255))).convert('RGB')
+        clay.append((label+' | height-derived normals + AO',im.crop(crop)))
+    sheet(clay,'clay_crossings.jpg',side=640)
+    print(json.dumps({k:metrics[k] for k in ['base_mean_srgb','normal_height_agreement_degrees',
+        'weave','roughness','ao','checks_passed']},indent=2))
+    print('Checks and previews saved in',CHECK)
+
+
+if __name__=='__main__':
+    main()

+Use case: photorealistic-natural.
+Asset type: original surface-detail source atlas for a survival game's dry split-reed floor material. This is NOT a finished woven mat; it is a sampling sheet of long flat reed ribbons.
+Primary request: a square, extremely detailed, orthographic straight-down scan of sixteen long, flattened split-reed / rush strips lying parallel horizontally, each going all the way from left to right edge. Fill the square. All fibres follow the horizontal long axis. Each ribbon has a slightly different natural cream, pale khaki, gray-beige or light tan pigment. Dry pale reed, desaturated, no green or orange. Subtle fibrous striations, occasional tiny transverse node creases and thin fraying at a few ribbon edges, fine flaking pith, slight abrasions, naturally uneven longitudinal pigment. Substantial fine real organic detail everywhere, not smooth or rendered plastic. Restrained variation, no distinctive dark marks, no holes, no rot.
+Composition: exactly overhead, flat, edge-to-edge crop of parallel horizontal split-reed ribbons; no perspective. No weave, no crossing ribbons, no borders, no binding, no props, no text.
+Lighting: diffuse cross-polarized scan, absolutely flat albedo-like surface, no directional lighting, no highlights, no cast shadows, no ambient occlusion between ribbons. Texture pigmentation only. Approximate mean sRGB 178,160,118. High resolution fine surface detail. Original invented material; do not reference any existing game.

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Build an original, periodic 40 x 40 split-reed 2/2 twill at native 2048.
+
+Requires Python 3, NumPy, SciPy and Pillow. No network access. All paths are
+relative to this script, and all outputs stay in this material directory.
+"""
+from pathlib import Path
+import hashlib
+import json
+import time
+
+import numpy as np
+from PIL import Image, ImageCms, PngImagePlugin
+from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter, map_coordinates
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+N = 2048
+STRIPS = 40
+TILE_M = 1.5
+HEIGHT_MM = 8.0
+SEED = 617803
+TARGET_RGB = np.array([178., 160., 118.], dtype=np.float32)
+SRGB = ImageCms.ImageCmsProfile(ImageCms.createProfile('sRGB')).tobytes()
+
+
+def smoothstep(a, b, x):
+    t = np.clip((x-a)/(b-a), 0., 1.)
+    return t*t*(3.-2.*t)
+
+
+def blur(a, sigma):
+    return gaussian_filter(a, sigma, mode='wrap')
+
+
+def noise(rng, shape, sigma):
+    a = blur(rng.standard_normal(shape, dtype=np.float32), sigma)
+    return (a - a.mean()) / max(float(a.std()), 1.e-6)
+
+
+def save_png(name, a, srgb=False, extra=None):
+    info = PngImagePlugin.PngInfo()
+    info.add_text('Material', 'Hand-plaited split reed; 2-over / 2-under twill; 40 strips per axis')
+    info.add_text('Physical size', '1.5 x 1.5 metres; repeat X and Y')
+    info.add_text('Color space', 'sRGB' if srgb else 'linear data; do not decode as sRGB')
+    for k, v in (extra or {}).items():
+        info.add_text(k, v)
+    kwargs = {'icc_profile': SRGB} if srgb else {}
+    Image.fromarray(a).save(ROOT / name, pnginfo=info, **kwargs)
+
+
+def rgb8(a):
+    return np.clip(np.rint(a), 0, 255).astype(np.uint8)
+
+
+def normal_from_height(h16):
+    """Central differences of the DELIVERED uint16 height; +Y is image-up."""
+    h = h16.astype(np.float32) * (HEIGHT_MM / 65535.)
+    px_mm = TILE_M * 1000. / N
+    dx = (np.roll(h, -1, 1) - np.roll(h, 1, 1)) / (2. * px_mm)
+    dy_down = (np.roll(h, -1, 0) - np.roll(h, 1, 0)) / (2. * px_mm)
+    normal = np.stack([-dx, dy_down, np.ones_like(h)], axis=-1)
+    normal /= np.linalg.norm(normal, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
+    return rgb8((normal * .5 + .5) * 255.)
+
+
+def make_atlas(rng, src):
+    """80 independent ribbon surfaces, 64 samples wide, 2048 along the fibre.
+
+    Three native-density source patches are blended around each ribbon's ring.
+    Source ribbon edges are excluded. Slow and cross-ribbon illumination is
+    removed before the residual is used as low-amplitude pigment detail.
+    Native pixel-scale procedural fibres provide independent relief and pigment.
+    """
+    bounds = np.array([0, 86, 173, 256, 338, 420, 502, 584,
+                       667, 749, 829, 909, 986, 1058, 1131, 1199, 1254])
+    bounds = bounds * src.shape[0] / 1254.
+    cy, ax = np.mgrid[:64, :N].astype(np.float32)
+    palettes = rng.normal(0, 4.8, 80)
+    palettes -= palettes.mean()
+    hue = rng.normal(0, 1.3, (80, 3)).astype(np.float32)
+    colour = np.empty((80, 64, N, 3), dtype=np.float32)
+    relief = np.empty((80, 64, N), dtype=np.float32)
+    fibre_saved = np.empty_like(relief)
+    for k in range(80):
+        detail = np.zeros((64, N), dtype=np.float32)
+        for p in range(3):
+            row = int(rng.integers(0, 16))
+            margin = 13.
+            ys = bounds[row] + margin + (cy+.5)/64.*(bounds[row+1]-bounds[row]-2*margin)
+            # Each patch is active only within +/- 683 pixels of its centre.
+            # Choose a 900 px periodic source track with a 200 px crossfade.
+            phase = rng.uniform(0, 900)
+            xs = (ax + phase) % 900
+            left = map_coordinates(src.mean(2), [ys, xs+160], order=1, mode='nearest')
+            right = map_coordinates(src.mean(2), [ys, xs+1060], order=1, mode='nearest')
+            w = smoothstep(0., 190., xs)
+            track = left*w + right*(1.-w)
+            # The right sample only contributes during the first 190 pixels,
+            # where xs+1060 <=1250. Adjacent ring endpoints remain continuous.
+            track -= blur(track, (7., 70.))
+            track -= track.mean(1, keepdims=True) * .45
+            # Raised-cosine partition of unity, periodic across output ends.
+            dist = ((ax - (p/3.*N) + N/2.) % N) - N/2.
+            weight = (1.+np.cos(np.pi*np.clip(dist/(N/3.), -1., 1.)))/2.
+            detail += track * weight
+        detail *= .65
+        coarse = noise(rng, (64, N), (1.8, 39.))
+        fine = noise(rng, (64, N), (.48, 12.))
+        fine2 = noise(rng, (64, N), (.85, 3.4))
+        drift = 1.4*np.sin(2*np.pi*ax/N*3+rng.uniform(0, 6.28))
+        drift += .55*np.sin(2*np.pi*ax/N*11+rng.uniform(0, 6.28))
+        fine = map_coordinates(fine, [(cy+drift)%64, ax], order=1, mode='grid-wrap')
+        broad = noise(rng, (64, N), (9., 110.))
+        local_tone = noise(rng, (64, N), (22., 135.))
+        fiber = .040*coarse + .026*fine + .013*fine2
+        # Gentle short transverse nodes and small edge splits, unique per ribbon.
+        node = np.zeros_like(detail)
+        splits = np.zeros_like(detail)
+        raised_fibre = np.zeros_like(detail)
+        for _ in range(int(rng.integers(1, 4))):
+            cx = rng.uniform(0, N)
+            distance = ((ax-cx+N/2.)%N)-N/2.
+            bend = (cy-32.) * rng.uniform(-.12, .12)
+            node += np.exp(-((distance+bend)/rng.uniform(.8, 1.9))**2/2.) * rng.uniform(.025, .065)
+        for _ in range(int(rng.integers(0, 4))):
+            cx = rng.uniform(0, N)
+            distance = ((ax-cx+N/2.)%N)-N/2.
+            taper = np.exp(-.5*(distance/rng.uniform(25, 100))**2)
+            edge_y = rng.choice([3.3, 59.7]) + 1.1*np.sin(distance/65.)
+            width = rng.uniform(.45, .85)
+            splits += np.exp(-.5*((cy-edge_y)/width)**2)*taper
+            outward = -1.7 if float(edge_y[0,0])<32 else 1.7
+            raised_fibre += np.exp(-.5*((cy-edge_y-outward)/.55)**2)*taper
+        # Stochastic lines are pigment/relief, not directional edge shading.
+        gray = detail + 1.7*coarse + 1.65*fine + .85*fine2 + 2.1*broad + 4.6*local_tone
+        gray += 12.*node - 9.*splits + 3.5*raised_fibre
+        colour[k] = TARGET_RGB + palettes[k]*np.array([1., .98, .85]) + hue[k]
+        colour[k] += gray[..., None] * np.array([1., .98, .85])
+        relief[k] = fiber + node - .24*splits + .18*raised_fibre + detail*.003
+        fibre_saved[k] = fiber
+    return colour.reshape(80*64, N, 3), relief.reshape(80*64, N), fibre_saved.reshape(80*64, N)
+
+
+def horizon_ao(h):
+    """Periodic heightfield horizon integration, twelve azimuths, eight radii."""
+    px_mm = TILE_M * 1000./N
+    ao = np.zeros_like(h)
+    for angle in np.arange(12)*2*np.pi/12:
+        slope = np.zeros_like(h)
+        seen = set()
+        for radius in [1, 2, 3, 5, 9, 16, 30, 58]:
+            dx, dy = int(round(np.cos(angle)*radius)), int(round(np.sin(angle)*radius))
+            if (dx, dy) in seen or (dx == 0 and dy == 0):
+                continue
+            seen.add((dx, dy))
+            sample = np.roll(h, (dy, dx), axis=(0, 1))
+            slope = np.maximum(slope, (sample-h)/(np.hypot(dx,dy)*px_mm))
+        ao += 1./(1.+slope*slope)
+    return np.clip(ao/12., 0., 1.)
+
+
+def main():
+    started = time.time()
+    rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
+    print('Building eighty native-density ribbon surfaces...', flush=True)
+    src = np.asarray(Image.open(ROOT/'source/generated_reed_atlas.png').convert('RGB'), dtype=np.float32)
+    atlas, grain_atlas, fine_atlas = make_atlas(rng, src)
+    # Retain full-resolution assembled source sheets for inspection and provenance.
+    save_png('source/composed_ribbon_surfaces.png', rgb8(atlas), srgb=True)
+    yy, xx = np.mgrid[:N, :N].astype(np.float32)
+    pitch = N/STRIPS
+    u = xx/pitch + .37
+    v = yy/pitch + .21
+    # Periodic deformations retain the 40-strip winding number on both axes.
+    # A broad warp and smaller irregularities avoid a machine-square basket grid.
+    u += .105*noise(rng, (N,N), 105.) + .035*noise(rng, (N,N), 23.)
+    v += .105*noise(rng, (N,N), 105.) + .035*noise(rng, (N,N), 23.)
+    column_offsets = rng.uniform(-.13, .13, 40).astype(np.float32)
+    row_offsets = rng.uniform(-.13, .13, 40).astype(np.float32)
+    u += map_coordinates(column_offsets, [(xx/pitch).ravel()], order=3, mode='grid-wrap').reshape(N,N)
+    v += map_coordinates(row_offsets, [(yy/pitch).ravel()], order=3, mode='grid-wrap').reshape(N,N)
+    ui, vi = np.floor(u).astype(np.int32), np.floor(v).astype(np.int32)
+    uf, vf = u-ui, v-vi
+    hcoords = [(vi%40)*64 + np.clip(vf*64, .51, 63.49), (u/40.*N)%N]
+    vcoords = [((ui%40)+40)*64 + np.clip(uf*64, .51, 63.49), (v/40.*N)%N]
+    print('Plaiting 40 x 40, with periodic width and alignment variation...', flush=True)
+    col_h = np.stack([map_coordinates(atlas[...,c], hcoords, order=1, mode='grid-wrap') for c in range(3)], -1)
+    col_v = np.stack([map_coordinates(atlas[...,c], vcoords, order=1, mode='grid-wrap') for c in range(3)], -1)
+    relief_h = map_coordinates(grain_atlas, hcoords, order=1, mode='grid-wrap')
+    relief_v = map_coordinates(grain_atlas, vcoords, order=1, mode='grid-wrap')
+    native_fibre_h = map_coordinates(fine_atlas, hcoords, order=1, mode='grid-wrap')
+    native_fibre_v = map_coordinates(fine_atlas, vcoords, order=1, mode='grid-wrap')
+    del atlas, grain_atlas, fine_atlas
+    wear = smoothstep(-.5, 2.0, .73*noise(rng, (N,N), 108.) + .27*noise(rng, (N,N), 39.))
+    # Ragged edges stay below a pixel on average; the weave remains intact.
+    edge_h = .25*noise(rng, (N,N), (4., 1.7)) + .23*noise(rng,(N,N), (8.,12.))
+    edge_v = .25*noise(rng, (N,N), (1.7,4.)) + .23*noise(rng,(N,N), (12.,8.))
+    dh = (np.minimum(vf,1.-vf)*pitch) - .64 + edge_h
+    dv = (np.minimum(uf,1.-uf)*pitch) - .64 + edge_v
+    cover_h = smoothstep(-.6,.6,dh)
+    cover_v = smoothstep(-.6,.6,dv)
+    phase_h = np.cos(np.pi/2.*(u-vi-1.))
+    phase_v = -np.cos(np.pi/2.*(ui-v))
+    arch_h = np.tanh(phase_h*1.7)/np.tanh(1.7)
+    arch_v = np.tanh(phase_v*1.7)/np.tanh(1.7)
+    thickness_h = rng.uniform(-.10,.10,40)[vi%40]
+    thickness_v = rng.uniform(-.10,.10,40)[ui%40]
+    # Flat ribbons, a very slight cross-section crown, soft bent exits at crossings.
+    base_z = 3.45 + .10*noise(rng,(N,N),52.)
+    h_raw = base_z + 1.28*arch_h + thickness_h + .13*np.sin(np.pi*vf)
+    v_raw = base_z + 1.28*arch_v + thickness_v + .13*np.sin(np.pi*uf)
+    h_raw -= .33*np.exp(-np.maximum(dh,0)/1.25)
+    v_raw -= .33*np.exp(-np.maximum(dv,0)/1.25)
+    h_raw += relief_h*(1.-.37*wear)
+    v_raw += relief_v*(1.-.37*wear)
+    ground_z = 1.32 + .035*noise(rng,(N,N),(.8,.8))
+    zh = ground_z + cover_h*(h_raw-ground_z)
+    zv = ground_z + cover_v*(v_raw-ground_z)
+    top_h = smoothstep(-.065,.065,zh-zv).astype(np.float32)
+    coverage = np.maximum(cover_h,cover_v)
+    height = blur(np.maximum(zh,zv).astype(np.float32), .39)
+    print('Computing heightfield cavity occlusion and native fine detail...', flush=True)
+    ao = horizon_ao(height)
+    cavity = smoothstep(.04,.39,1.-ao)
+    grit_rng = rng.random((N,N), dtype=np.float32)
+    grit = blur((grit_rng > .987).astype(np.float32),.52)*2.5*cavity
+    dust = cavity*(.28+.25*smoothstep(-1.,1.,noise(rng,(N,N),5.)))
+    base = col_h*top_h[...,None] + col_v*(1.-top_h[...,None])
+    del col_h, col_v
+    # Only real deposited dust/grit affects albedo at crossings. AO is separate.
+    base = base*coverage[...,None] + np.array([158.,145.,113.])*(1.-coverage[...,None])
+    base += wear[...,None]*np.array([3.8,4.1,4.8])
+    base = base*(1.-dust[...,None]*.10) + np.array([168.,154.,122.])*dust[...,None]*.10
+    base -= grit[...,None]*np.array([12.,11.,8.])
+    height += grit*.07
+    base += TARGET_RGB - base.mean((0,1))
+    roughness = .827 + .017*noise(rng,(N,N),9.) - .115*wear + .041*cavity
+    roughness += .010*noise(rng,(N,N),.65)
+    roughness = np.clip(roughness,.66,.92)
+    h16 = np.rint(np.clip(height/HEIGHT_MM,0,1)*65535).astype(np.uint16)
+    # Recompute AO after the tiny grit relief, using the quantized delivered height.
+    ao = horizon_ao(h16.astype(np.float32)*HEIGHT_MM/65535.)
+    save_png('mat_base.png',rgb8(base),srgb=True)
+    save_png('mat_height.png',h16,extra={'Height scale':'0..65535 = 0..8 millimetres; white is high'})
+    save_png('mat_normal.png',normal_from_height(h16),extra={'Normal convention':'OpenGL: +X right, +Y image-up, +Z out; central differences with wrap'})
+    save_png('mat_orm.png',rgb8(np.stack([ao,roughness,np.zeros_like(ao)],-1)*255),
+             extra={'Channels':'R ambient occlusion; G roughness; B metalness (zero)'})
+    # These are diagnostic fields, not additional engine textures.
+    np.savez_compressed(ROOT/'source/derivation.npz',u=u.astype(np.float32),v=v.astype(np.float32),
+        horizontal_top=top_h,wear=wear.astype(np.float32),coverage=coverage.astype(np.float32),
+        native_fibre_mm=(native_fibre_h*top_h+native_fibre_v*(1.-top_h)).astype(np.float32))
+    meta = {'seed':SEED,'resolution':[N,N],'strips_per_axis':STRIPS,'tile_metres':[TILE_M,TILE_M],
+        'pitch_mm':TILE_M*1000/STRIPS,'pixel_mm':TILE_M*1000/N,'height_range_mm':HEIGHT_MM,
+        'used_height_mm':[float(h16.min()*HEIGHT_MM/65535),float(h16.max()*HEIGHT_MM/65535)],
+        'source_size':list(Image.open(ROOT/'source/generated_reed_atlas.png').size),
+        'source_sha256':hashlib.sha256((ROOT/'source/generated_reed_atlas.png').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+        'normal_method':'wrapped central differences of delivered uint16; encode normalized (-dH/dx,+dH/drow,1)',
+        'build_seconds':round(time.time()-started,2)}
+    (ROOT/'source/build_metadata.json').write_text(json.dumps(meta,indent=2)+'\n')
+    print(json.dumps(meta,indent=2),flush=True)
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    main()

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..75b816076c4888e0f796af4b5d48f3c3605187fc

+++ b/assets/source/core/structures/thatch/tex/mat/check_material.py

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Validate the delivered maps and regenerate every visual check, offline."""
+from pathlib import Path
+import hashlib
+import json
+import struct
+
+import numpy as np
+from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFont
+from scipy.ndimage import gaussian_filter
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+CHECK = ROOT/'checks'
+N = 2048
+TILE_M = 1.5
+HEIGHT_MM = 8.
+NAMES = ['base','height','normal','orm']
+
+
+def save_view(path, array):
+    Image.fromarray(np.clip(np.rint(array),0,255).astype(np.uint8)).save(CHECK/path)
+
+
+def sheet(items, path, columns=2, side=768):
+    rows = (len(items)+columns-1)//columns
+    canvas = Image.new('RGB',(columns*side, rows*(side+36)),(25,26,25))
+    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(canvas)
+    try:
+        font = ImageFont.truetype('/System/Library/Fonts/Supplemental/Arial.ttf',17)
+    except OSError:
+        font = ImageFont.load_default()
+    for idx,(label,im) in enumerate(items):
+        x, y = (idx%columns)*side,(idx//columns)*(side+36)
+        draw.text((x+12,y+10),label,fill=(234,233,223),font=font)
+        canvas.paste(im.convert('RGB').resize((side,side),Image.Resampling.LANCZOS),(x,y+36))
+    canvas.save(CHECK/path,quality=94,subsampling=0)
+
+
+def seam_stats(a):
+    """All errors in encoded 8-bit-equivalent units, even for uint16 height."""
+    a = a.astype(np.float64)
+    if a.ndim == 2:
+        a = a[...,None]
+    result = {}
+    for axis,label in [(1,'left_right'),(0,'top_bottom')]:
+        wrap = np.abs(np.take(a,0,axis)-np.take(a,-1,axis))
+        interior = np.abs(np.diff(a,axis=axis))
+        other = 0 if axis == 1 else 1
+        edge_means = interior.mean(axis=(other,2))
+        mean = float(wrap.mean())
+        p95 = float(np.percentile(edge_means,95))
+        result[label] = {'wrap_mean':mean,'interior_mean':float(interior.mean()),
+            'interior_line_p50':float(np.median(edge_means)),
+            'interior_line_p95':p95,'wrap_to_interior_p95':mean/max(p95,1.e-8),
+            'wrap_pixel_p99':float(np.percentile(wrap,99)),
+            'interior_pixel_p99':float(np.percentile(interior,99)),
+            'within_interior_p95':bool(mean<=p95)}
+    return result
+
+
+def decode_srgb(s):
+    return np.where(s<=.04045,s/12.92,((s+.055)/1.055)**2.4)
+
+
+def encode_srgb(l):
+    l = np.maximum(l,0)
+    return np.where(l<=.0031308,12.92*l,1.055*l**(1/2.4)-.055)
+
+
+def main():
+    CHECK.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    maps = {name:np.array(Image.open(ROOT/f'mat_{name}.png')) for name in NAMES}
+    formats = {}
+    for name in NAMES:
+        path = ROOT/f'mat_{name}.png'
+        with Image.open(path) as im:
+            ihdr = struct.unpack('>IIBBBBB',path.read_bytes()[16:29])
+            formats[name] = {'size':list(im.size),'mode':im.mode,'bit_depth':ihdr[2],
+                'png_color_type':ihdr[3],'has_icc_profile':bool(im.info.get('icc_profile')),
+                'sha256':hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()}
+            assert im.size == (N,N), f'{name}: wrong dimensions'
+            assert ihdr[2] == (16 if name=='height' else 8), f'{name}: wrong bit depth'
+            assert ihdr[3] == (0 if name=='height' else 2), f'{name}: wrong channels'
+            assert bool(im.info.get('icc_profile')) == (name=='base'), f'{name}: colour profile'
+    base = maps['base'].astype(np.float32)
+    height = maps['height'].astype(np.float64) * HEIGHT_MM / 65535.
+    normal_raw = maps['normal'].astype(np.float64)/127.5-1.
+    lengths = np.linalg.norm(normal_raw,axis=-1)
+    normal = normal_raw/lengths[...,None]
+    orm = maps['orm'].astype(np.float64)/255.
+    # Independent reconstruction: pad the saved height, differentiate, normalize.
+    hp = np.pad(height,1,mode='wrap')
+    px_mm = TILE_M*1000/N
+    dx = (hp[1:-1,2:]-hp[1:-1,:-2])/(2*px_mm)
+    drow = (hp[2:,1:-1]-hp[:-2,1:-1])/(2*px_mm)
+    expected = np.stack([-dx,drow,np.ones_like(dx)],-1)
+    expected /= np.linalg.norm(expected,axis=-1,keepdims=True)
+    angular = np.degrees(np.arccos(np.clip((normal*expected).sum(-1),-1,1)))
+    directx = expected.copy()
+    directx[...,1] *= -1
+    wrong_g = np.degrees(np.arccos(np.clip((normal*directx).sum(-1),-1,1)))
+    assert angular.max()<.45, 'Normal does not agree with height within 8-bit quantization'
+    assert np.all(maps['orm'][...,2]==0),'Nonzero metalness'
+    mean = base.astype(np.float64).mean((0,1))
+    assert np.max(np.abs(mean-[178,160,118]))<.1,'Base colour mean missed target'
+    seam = {name:seam_stats(a*(255./65535.) if name=='height' else a) for name,a in maps.items()}
+    assert all(s['within_interior_p95'] for m in seam.values() for s in m.values()), 'A border exceeds normal interior variation'
+
+    fields = np.load(ROOT/'source/derivation.npz')
+    u,v = fields['u'],fields['v']
+    ui,vi = np.floor(u).astype(int),np.floor(v).astype(int)
+    label = (vi%40)*40+(ui%40)
+    centre_distance = (u-ui-.5)**2+(v-vi-.5)**2
+    mins = np.full(1600,np.inf)
+    np.minimum.at(mins,label.ravel(),centre_distance.ravel())
+    centres = centre_distance==mins[label]
+    expected_top = ((ui-vi)%4)<2
+    wrong_centres = np.count_nonzero((fields['horizontal_top'][centres]>.5)!=expected_top[centres])
+    assert np.count_nonzero(centres)==1600 and wrong_centres==0,'Incorrect 2/2 crossing order'
+    # Central differences on the unwrapped coordinates, excluding wrap jumps.
+    du = np.diff(u,axis=1)
+    dv = np.diff(v,axis=0)
+    assert du.min()>0 and dv.min()>0,'Warp folds or reverses strips'
+    wear = fields['wear'].ravel()[::7]
+    wear_correlation = float(np.corrcoef(wear,orm[...,1].ravel()[::7])[0,1])
+    assert wear_correlation<-.6,'Worn areas should be smoother'
+    cavity = gaussian_filter(height,3.,mode='wrap')-height
+    cavity_ao = float(orm[...,0][cavity>.2].mean())
+    flat_ao = float(orm[...,0][np.abs(cavity)<.015].mean())
+    assert cavity_ao<flat_ao,'Cavity AO appears inverted'
+    metrics = {'physical':{'tile_metres':[TILE_M,TILE_M],'resolution':[N,N],
+        'strip_count_each_axis':40,'nominal_strip_pitch_mm':37.5,'pixel_mm':px_mm,
+        'full_height_range_mm':HEIGHT_MM,'actual_height_min_max_mm':[float(height.min()),float(height.max())]},
+        'formats':formats,'base_mean_srgb':mean.tolist(),
+        'base_channel_min_max':[[int(base[...,i].min()),int(base[...,i].max())] for i in range(3)],
+        'wrap_seams_8bit_equivalent':seam,
+        'normal_height_agreement_degrees':{'mean':float(angular.mean()),'p99':float(np.percentile(angular,99)),
+            'max':float(angular.max()),'wrong_green_convention_mean':float(wrong_g.mean())},
+        'decoded_normal_length':{'mean':float(lengths.mean()),'max_error':float(np.max(np.abs(lengths-1)))},
+        'weave':{'verified_crossings':int(np.count_nonzero(centres)),'wrong_crossings':int(wrong_centres),
+            'pattern':'horizontal on top iff (column - row) mod 4 is 0 or 1; both axes 40',
+            'minimum_grid_jacobian_diagonals':[float(du.min()),float(dv.min())]},
+        'roughness':{'min':float(orm[...,1].min()),'max':float(orm[...,1].max()),
+            'mean':float(orm[...,1].mean()),'wear_correlation':wear_correlation},
+        'ao':{'min':float(orm[...,0].min()),'mean':float(orm[...,0].mean()),
+            'cavity_mean':cavity_ao,'flat_mean':flat_ao},
+        'metalness_nonzero_pixels':int(np.count_nonzero(maps['orm'][...,2])),
+        'native_detail':{'procedural_fibre_rms_mm':float(fields['native_fibre_mm'].std()),
+            'base_highpass_rms_1px':float((base-gaussian_filter(base,(1.,1.,0.),mode='wrap')).std())},
+        'checks_passed':True}
+    (CHECK/'metrics.json').write_text(json.dumps(metrics,indent=2)+'\n')
+    display = {name:Image.fromarray((a/257.).astype(np.uint8) if name=='height' else a) for name,a in maps.items()}
+    sheet([(f'mat_{name} | '+('sRGB' if name=='base' else 'linear data'),im) for name,im in display.items()],
+          'layers.jpg')
+    offsets=[]
+    for name,a in maps.items():
+        off=np.roll(a,(N//2,N//2),axis=(0,1))
+        Image.fromarray(off).save(CHECK/f'{name}_half_offset.png')
+        off_view=Image.fromarray((off/257.).astype(np.uint8) if name=='height' else off)
+        offsets.append((f'{name} | wrap offset 1024,1024',off_view))
+    sheet(offsets,'offsets.jpg')
+    tile=Image.fromarray(np.tile(maps['base'],(3,3,1)))
+    tile.save(CHECK/'base_tiled_3x3.png')
+    tile.resize((1920,1920),Image.Resampling.LANCZOS).save(CHECK/'tilings.jpg',quality=95,subsampling=0)
+    crop=(704,704,1344,1344)
+    display['base'].crop(crop).save(CHECK/'base_crop_1to1.png')
+    display['normal'].crop(crop).save(CHECK/'normal_crop_1to1.png')
+    sheet([(f'{name} | 1:1, x704 y704, 640px',display[name].crop(crop)) for name in ['base','normal']],
+          'detail_1to1.png',side=640)
+    sheet([(f'{name} | aligned 640px crop',display[name].crop(crop)) for name in NAMES],
+          'alignment.jpg',side=640)
+    # Relighting is diagnostic. The delivered base contains none of this light.
+    linear=decode_srgb(base/255.)
+    lighting=[]
+    lights=[('upper left',[-.63,.45,.64]),('lower right',[.57,-.55,.61]),('upper grazing',[.1,.86,.5])]
+    for label,vec in lights:
+        light=np.array(vec)/np.linalg.norm(vec)
+        ndotl=np.maximum(0.,(normal*light).sum(-1))
+        shade=.28*orm[...,0]+.91*ndotl*(.75+.25*orm[...,0])
+        lit=encode_srgb(linear*shade[...,None])
+        lit=Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.clip(np.rint(lit*255),0,255)))
+        lit.save(CHECK/('relit_'+label.replace(' ','_')+'.png'))
+        lighting.append((label+' | full tile',lit))
+    lighting.extend([(label+' | 1:1 detail',im.crop(crop)) for label,im in lighting.copy()])
+    sheet(lighting,'relighting.jpg',columns=3,side=640)
+    # Neutral clay views expose the crossing shape independently of pigmentation.
+    clay=[]
+    for label,vec in lights[:2]:
+        light=np.array(vec)/np.linalg.norm(vec)
+        shade=.25*orm[...,0]+.72*np.maximum(0.,(normal*light).sum(-1))
+        im=Image.fromarray(np.uint8(np.clip(encode_srgb(shade)*220,0,255))).convert('RGB')
+        clay.append((label+' | height-derived normals + AO',im.crop(crop)))
+    sheet(clay,'clay_crossings.jpg',side=640)
+    print(json.dumps({k:metrics[k] for k in ['base_mean_srgb','normal_height_agreement_degrees',
+        'weave','roughness','ao','checks_passed']},indent=2))
+    print('Checks and previews saved in',CHECK)
+
+
+if __name__=='__main__':
+    main()

+Use case: photorealistic-natural.
+Asset type: original surface-detail source atlas for a survival game's dry split-reed floor material. This is NOT a finished woven mat; it is a sampling sheet of long flat reed ribbons.
+Primary request: a square, extremely detailed, orthographic straight-down scan of sixteen long, flattened split-reed / rush strips lying parallel horizontally, each going all the way from left to right edge. Fill the square. All fibres follow the horizontal long axis. Each ribbon has a slightly different natural cream, pale khaki, gray-beige or light tan pigment. Dry pale reed, desaturated, no green or orange. Subtle fibrous striations, occasional tiny transverse node creases and thin fraying at a few ribbon edges, fine flaking pith, slight abrasions, naturally uneven longitudinal pigment. Substantial fine real organic detail everywhere, not smooth or rendered plastic. Restrained variation, no distinctive dark marks, no holes, no rot.
+Composition: exactly overhead, flat, edge-to-edge crop of parallel horizontal split-reed ribbons; no perspective. No weave, no crossing ribbons, no borders, no binding, no props, no text.
+Lighting: diffuse cross-polarized scan, absolutely flat albedo-like surface, no directional lighting, no highlights, no cast shadows, no ambient occlusion between ribbons. Texture pigmentation only. Approximate mean sRGB 178,160,118. High resolution fine surface detail. Original invented material; do not reference any existing game.
